# Late start — rest-of-day P&L by switch-on time (821 days 2022-12-27..2026-10-07; 56 NDX-priced 2026)

Engine: runDay5m opts.actionStartMin (empty book at the start, earlier bars build the signal only). Roster = buildRuns() at 61da506
(96 variants), optsFor + ladder opens + 1m sub-bar cover fill + intraday IV, as build-backtest-baselines. A late start skips the
morning entirely: its P&L is rest-of-day only, nothing carried. 09:30 = control (actionStartMin unset).

**Control check vs backtest-baselines-combined.json:** 96/96 variants match exactly on total, avg/day, worst, maxDD30, locked %, end floor, opens/day.

Columns: worst day, maxDD30, IF avg (intraday book floor avg over bars holding positions, traded days), IF>=0% (share of those bars with floor >= 0),
end floor (avg EOD book floor, traded days), locked % (traded days ending floor > 0), uncov/d (uncovered positions at EOD per day), opens/d, avg/d, total;
then Δ vs that variant's 09:30 control on worst, maxDD30, IF>=0% (pts), total. Per variant, never averaged.

"full-day" columns = the same variant at the 09:30 control on the same days (full-day P&L, includes the morning) for reference.
Ref 10-02 = what the 2026-10-02 run found (765d NQ-priced set, roster of that day).
